# MaterialX 1.39.4 Audit Notes

This file is the continuity log for the MaterialX 1.39.4 audit.
Every session must read this file before continuing the audit.

## Constraints

- Audit every implemented MaterialX node with no priority shortcuts and no skips.
- Read the seven MaterialX 1.39.4 markdown specification documents.
- Download and inspect the upstream MaterialX MDL implementation files.
- Compare specification, MDL implementation, and local implementation line by line.
- Check every socket, default value, reference, inheritance path, `sample`, `eval`, `pdf`, and `le`.
- Silent fallback on invalid connections or references is forbidden. Unsupported VDF, SSS, measured EDF, displacement, animated image, cubic image filter, and spectral-rendering requirements may warn instead.
- The audit scope includes loader/compile/runtime, `MtlxMaterial`, integrators, light tree, opacity and alpha handling, passthrough, emitter gating, shading vertex data, roughness/alpha handling, tests, and stale or unnecessary comments.
- Test auditing must check not only coverage but also whether helpers, expected values, assertions, and failure cases themselves match the specification and MDL/reference implementations line by line.
- Current color-space support limited to sRGB and linear is accepted until the future OCIO task.
- Volume and light nodes may remain unsupported for now.
- Audit-note updates must not be simple blind tail appends. Preserve all required facts, add new notes in the appropriate section, and update existing status/index entries when their state changes.

## Current Audit Index

Use this section first when resuming work. The chronological log below is retained for provenance, including older "Follow-up required" notes that were later superseded by detailed compare entries and fixes. An item is currently open only if it appears in "Current Open Items" below or is added by a later chronological entry after this index is updated.

### Current Open Items

- None currently recorded. If future audit work finds a new issue or follow-up, add it here and update the relevant completed entry instead of only appending a tail note.

### Completed Coverage Index

- Specification markdown: all seven MaterialX 1.39.4 markdown files listed above have been read line by line at least once. GeomExts, Supplement, and Proposals were rechecked in the later non-node specification pass.
- MDL and generated reference files: all MDL files listed above, the generated MDL implementation `.mtlx` files, and the additional generated GLSL/OSL/C++ reference files named in the chronological entries have been read and compared in the relevant node passes.
- Loader and document handling: `types.rs`, `parser.rs`, `resolver.rs`, `library.rs`, `flatten.rs`, and `build.rs` have detailed passes recorded for strict parsing, nodedef resolution, inheritance, XInclude, nodegraph materialization, tokens, file paths, image defaults, colorspace warnings, unsupported geometry filename tokens, and no silent invalid-reference fallback.
- StandardNodes: `stdlib_defs.mtlx` lines 1-5110 have been covered across detailed passes. Image/tiled/hextiled/triplanar nodes, constants, ramps/splits, geometry/application/NPR nodes, math/matrix/transform nodes, normal/bump/heighttonormal, adjustments, compositing, conditionals, convert/combine/extract/separate, blur, logical nodes, and organization `dot` have explicit comparison entries.
- PBR and NPR libraries: `pbrlib_defs.mtlx` lines 1-462, `pbrlib_ng.mtlx`, `nprlib_defs.mtlx`, and `nprlib_ng.mtlx` have detailed comparison entries for BSDF/EDF/VDF/shader/light/displacement nodes, closure combinators, utility nodes, `viewdirection`, `facingratio`, and `gooch_shade`.
- Runtime and BSDF math: detailed entries cover diffuse/translucent/Gooch, dielectric/conductor/generalized Schlick, sheen/Zeltner, Chiang hair, EDFs, PBR utilities, procedural noise, hextile, ramp nodegraphs, matrix arithmetic, conversion, round, smoothstep, geometric normal transforms, and unsupported blur/dynamic heighttonormal behavior.
- Integration surface: detailed entries cover `MtlxMaterial`, top-level `Material`, PT/NEE/MIS integrators, lights, light-tree builder/importance/lobe/traversal, alpha-test/any-hit traversal, `is_pure_emitter`, `may_emit`, `max_emission`, `target_triangle`, reverse PDFs, and `ShadingVertex` construction.
- `src/scene.rs` current-file reconciliation: current file lines 1-1468 have been reread after recent edits for MaterialX scratch sizing, alpha-test/any-hit traversal, `ShadingVertex` front-face/ng/ns/frame/object transforms, emissive triangle registration, area-light PDFs, and scene tests.
- `src/material/mod.rs` and `src/material/mtlx_material.rs` current-file reconciliation: current files lines 1-606 and 1-562 have been reread after recent edits for Material dispatch, `MtlxMaterial::{sample,eval,pdf,le}`, precompute/scratch lifetime, front/back graph selection, thin-walled transmission, opacity/any-hit/passthrough, emitter flags, and light-tree precompute/importance.
- `src/material/mtlx/spec_tests.rs` current-file reconciliation: current file lines 1-9077 have been reread after recent edits. Test helpers, expected values, assertions, unsupported-policy tests, and failure cases were checked against the MaterialX specification, stdlib/pbrlib nodedefs, and MDL/generated reference behavior rather than only counted as coverage.
- Post-completion line-count reconciliation: the completed index was checked against current file line counts. Ranges that were not explicit in the latest current-file entries were reread: `parser.rs` lines 1259-1451 of 1451, `resolver.rs` lines 151-191 of 191, `flatten.rs` lines 2191-3253 of 3253, `build.rs` lines 561-698 of 698, `compiled.rs` lines 1139-1155 of 1155, `compile.rs` lines 6296-6332 of 6332, `runtime.rs` lines 5166-5178 of 5178, `generalized_schlick.rs` lines 404-408 of 408, and `chiang_hair.rs` line 502 of 502. No new unaddressed MaterialX violation, silent invalid-reference fallback, incorrect test expectation, or redundant comment requiring removal was found in those rereads.
- Final verification: after the current-file reconciliation passes, `cargo fmt`, `cargo check`, and the full `cargo test` suite completed successfully.
- Post-completion targeted verification: `cargo test scene_loader::mtlx_loader`, `cargo test material::mtlx`, and `cargo test bsdf::mtlx` completed successfully after the line-count reconciliation.
- Comment-rule cleanup: several passes removed comments that merely restated test names or code; scratch lifetime and MDL attenuation comments were kept only where they document non-obvious behavior.
- Audit note organization: the current active worklist is now separated from the historical chronological log, and stale early follow-up bullets are explicitly retained only as provenance unless repeated in Current Open Items.

### Accepted Unsupported Or Warning Policies

- Color spaces beyond sRGB/linear are temporarily accepted as warning paths until the OCIO task.
- Volume and light nodes may remain unsupported for now; volume material paths warn or become transparent/pass-through under the accepted policy.
- SSS may fall back to Burley diffuse for now, with recorded warning/coverage.
- Measured EDF, displacement, animated images, cubic filtering, spectral rendering requirements, blur convolution, and dynamic height-to-normal sample-grid behavior are allowed to warn or error explicitly. They must not silently approximate an invalid connection or reference.

The previous runtime treated the scalar height value itself as UV derivatives, which made constant nonzero heights produce a false slope. Continued `convert` channel-size comparison. The StandardNodes conversion table says extra channels are zero except the fourth channel, which is one, and same-size channel conversions copy channels directly. Local color3-to-color4 already matched this, but vector3-to-vector4 wrote `w = 0`, and vector4/color4 cross-conversions dropped the source fourth channel. Runtime now writes `w = 1` only for dimension expansion and preserves all four channels for same-size color4/vector4 conversions, with regression tests for both cases.

Local runtime matches the MDL definition `t*t*(3-2*t)` with `t` clamped to 0-1 for supported low < high inputs; equal or reversed bounds remain undefined by MDL, so no local silent fallback change was made.

  - `src/bsdf/mtlx/*.rs` (2016 lines total)
  - `src/integrator/*.rs` (1916 lines total)
  - `src/light_tree/*.rs` (1686 lines total)
  - `src/scene.rs`
  - `src/main.rs`
  - `src/scenes/scene_45.rs`
  - `README.md`
  - `lib/materialx/libraries/**/*.mtlx`

## Findings

- Closed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/types.rs` numeric array parsing rejects malformed elements through `Option` collection rather than dropping them. String arrays now follow the MaterialX escape convention and parse an empty literal as an empty array.
- Closed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/parser.rs` uses required attributes and structured parser errors for malformed root version, missing names/types/node/nodedef/socket attributes, malformed booleans, invalid geomprop metadata, ambiguous input/output bindings, malformed sockets, and XInclude cycle/missing-`href` cases. Remaining `unwrap_or` uses are scoped inheritance defaults for optional `fileprefix`/colorspace paths rather than invalid-reference fallback.
- Closed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/library.rs` now errors on missing/cyclic nodedef inheritance, missing required standard library files, and unknown inputs during nodedef matching. Requested versions do not fall back to default versions, and version matching now treats an omitted minor component as zero as required by the MaterialX nodedef version format.
- Closed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs` now errors on missing interfaces, missing required nodedef/nodegraph inputs, invalid typed literals/defaults, unresolved nodegraph implementations, missing nodegraph outputs, and unresolved flat outputs. Empty shader bindings remain explicit empty shader values, volume materials still warn and become passthrough by accepted policy, and ordinary unconnected non-shader primitive inputs use the MaterialX zero/identity default rule. Local nodedef version matching now treats an omitted minor component as zero.
- Closed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/build.rs` image and UDIM load failures remain warnings because MaterialX image nodes define `default` fallback behavior for unresolved files/layers. Unknown or approximated image colorspaces now warn before using the temporary sRGB/linear-only fallback; this is accepted until the future OCIO integration.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx_material.rs` front/back queries now conservatively combine `may_emit`, `max_emission`, and `has_alpha_test`; `any_hit`, `precompute_shading`, `sample`, `eval`, `pdf`, `le`, and light-tree precompute use the active front/back compiled graph selected by `front_face`.
- Closed: `src/material/mod.rs` intentionally keeps `is_pure_emitter` true only for native `Emissive`; `Mtlx` is not treated as a pure emitter so NEE/MIS direct-light sampling remains enabled for conservative MaterialX emitters.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` closure compilation no longer silently converts invalid literal/string/geomprop/non-closure nodes to `ClosureNode::Zero`; those paths return `CompileError::Unsupported`. Empty shader sockets and explicit VDF/displacement unsupported paths remain intentional zero/ignored closures under the accepted volume/displacement policy. `dot` empty shader passthrough matches the stdlib empty shader default.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` image input colorspace and `transformcolor` unsupported spaces now warn before using the temporary sRGB/linear-only fallback or unchanged color behavior.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` `worleynoise*/unifiednoise*.style` now accepts only the MaterialX enum values `0/Distance` and `1/Solid`; unknown/static-invalid/dynamic values error.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` `image.filtertype=cubic` and `triplanarblend.filtertype=cubic` now warn before using linear filtering; unknown filter strings error.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` geometric/application defaults match current supported scene data: `geomcolor` returns the MaterialX default black when no vertex color exists, `frame` returns nodedef default 1, and `time` returns nodedef default 0 until host frame/time plumbing exists.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` image instructions do not apply `color_space` at sample time because `build.rs`/`Texture::from_file_with_color_space` decode image pixels at load time. Unsupported/non-OCIO colorspaces warn at load/compile boundaries under the accepted sRGB/linear-only constraint.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` numeric math no longer uses defensive finite fallbacks for `modulo`, `sqrt`, `ln`, `asin`, or `acos`; zero/out-of-domain inputs propagate native floating-point NaN/inf behavior matching generated MDL/native math expectations.
- Closed: `src/bsdf/mtlx/chiang_hair.rs` clamps `cuticle_angle` to the PBRSpec `[0, 1]` range before mapping 0.5 to no tilt, and tests now use in-range values.
- Closed historical candidate: the earlier note that `src/material/mtlx/spec_tests.rs` contained only targeted regression coverage was superseded by later node-by-node and current-file reconciliation passes. The current test file lines 1-9077 were reread with helpers, expected values, assertions, unsupported-policy tests, and failure cases checked against the specification, stdlib/pbrlib nodedefs, and MDL/generated reference behavior.
- Fixed: `src/integrator/nee.rs` and `src/integrator/mis.rs` sampled the BSDF before direct-light estimation and skipped direct-light sampling when the sampled branch had `DELTA`. This made direct lighting depend on the randomly selected BSDF branch and under-sampled mixed MaterialX closures containing both delta and smooth components. Direct-light estimation now runs before BSDF sampling and only skips pure emitter materials.
- Closed: `src/material/mtlx_material.rs` light-tree precompute no longer uses a diffuse-only proxy. Runtime builds a closure summary that preserves diffuse, glossy, and BTDF lobes for supported MaterialX closures, and `MtlxMaterial::light_tree_importance` sums all three precompute lobes.
- Fixed: `src/scene.rs` `update_mtlx_scratch_capacity` only considered the front compiled MaterialX graph. Back-side MaterialX graphs can have more registers, so `mtlx_num_locals` could under-report capacity.
- Fixed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/parser.rs` silently accepted malformed documents by defaulting a bad/missing root `version` and dropping malformed `typedef`, `geompropdef`, `nodedef`, `implementation`, `input`, and `output` elements through `Option`/`filter_map`. These now return `ParseError::Structure` with the source path and missing/invalid attribute.
- Fixed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/library.rs` silently tolerated nodedef inheritance errors and ambiguous matching. Missing or cyclic inherited nodedefs now return `ParseError::Structure`; missing required standard library files now return `ParseError::Io`; requested nodedef versions no longer fall back to default versions; unknown inputs no longer match a nodedef.
- Fixed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs` silently accepted invalid literals, missing interface bindings, unresolved disabled-node nodedefs, missing nodegraph implementations, and unknown nodegraph/nodedef inputs. Invalid numeric/vector/color literals now error instead of becoming strings or empty values; missing output interfaces now error; non-primitive nodes without resolved nodedef/implementation no longer fall through to primitive materialization. The common `disable` control input is excluded from nodedef socket matching.
- Fixed: `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/build.rs` image colorspace collection now warns when non-sRGB/linear spaces are approximated or treated as linear under the temporary sRGB/linear-only project constraint.
- Fixed: `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` numeric math no longer clamps or substitutes finite values where MDL uses native math. `modulo` now matches `core::mx_mod` (`x - y * floor(x/y)`), including negative divisors and NaN propagation for zero divisors; `asin`, `acos`, `sqrt`, and `ln` now call native functions without domain clamping.
- Fixed: `src/bsdf/mtlx/chiang_hair.rs` now clamps `cuticle_angle` to the PBRSpec-defined `[0, 1]` range before mapping 0.5 to no tilt, and its tests no longer use out-of-range cuticle values.
- Fixed: `src/material/mtlx` `hextilednormalmap` no longer treats a missing or non-color texture as an implicit flat normal. The instruction now carries the `default`, `normal`, `tangent`, and `bitangent` sockets; missing texture returns `default` like MDL `mx_hextilednormalmap_vector3`; valid textures use per-tile rotated tangent/bitangent vectors, strength, and gradient-based normal blending.
- Fixed: `surface_unlit` is now preserved as `FlatNodeKind::SurfaceUnlit` instead of being flattened into the same kind as `surface`. A `surface_unlit` node with no explicit inputs now compiles with the MaterialX defaults (`emission=1`, white color, no transmission, opacity 1, thin-walled) instead of becoming a non-emissive regular surface.
- Fixed: PBR roughness utility nodes now match MDL and pbrlib nodedefs. `roughness_anisotropy` uses the MDL clamp/aspect formula, `glossiness_anisotropy` uses nodedef default glossiness 1.0 and evaluates through `roughness_anisotropy(1 - glossiness, anisotropy)`, and `roughness_dual` now accepts vector2 input with negative-y mirroring and per-channel squaring/clamping.
- Closed: `splittb` has a known upstream conflict: StandardNodes/GLSL/OSL describe/use V/Y, while MaterialX 1.39.4 generated MDL uses `texcoord.x`. Per this audit's MDL-equivalence requirement, local runtime follows the MDL x-axis step behavior and is covered by `spec_splittb_uses_mdl_x_axis_step`.
- Fixed: `ramplr`/`ramptb` and `splitlr`/`splittb` compile defaults now use zero for the right/bottom value inputs when omitted, matching stdlib nodedefs and MDL. They previously used `one_param`, making omitted right/bottom values silently become 1.0.
- Fixed: `worleynoise2d/3d.style` and `unifiednoise2d/3d.style` now parse the MaterialX integer enum values `0=Distance` and `1=Solid`, while still accepting string enum names for compatibility. Invalid style values and dynamic enum connections now error instead of silently becoming Distance. `unifiednoise2d/3d.type` now also requires a static integer enum value and rejects dynamic/type-mismatched inputs instead of defaulting to Perlin.
- Fixed: boolean logical pattern nodes (`and`, `or`, `xor`, `not`) and `geompropvalue` integer/boolean defaults were unreachable because `emit_pattern` rejected Boolean/Integer output types before their match arms. These now compile through explicit early handlers. Unsupported string/filename pattern outputs now error instead of being treated as float.
- Fixed: `geomcolor` with no vertex color data now returns the MaterialX nodedef default black instead of white.
- Fixed: `safepower` now compiles and evaluates as `sign(in1) * pow(abs(in1), in2)`, matching the StandardNodes definition. `floor`/`ceil`/`round` integer output variants now compile by applying the float operation and converting to integer, matching generated MDL `int(...)` implementations.
- Fixed: `magnitude`, `dotproduct`, and `distance` compile paths now preserve vector2/vector3/vector4 socket dimensions instead of forcing vector3. Runtime already handled all dimensions; the compiler now emits the correct instruction type.
- Fixed: `transformnormal` now uses the MaterialX nodedef default input `(0,0,1)` when `in` is omitted. It previously shared the transformpoint/vector zero default and could silently produce a zero normal-derived result.
- Fixed: `transpose` and `invertmatrix` matrix outputs are no longer rejected by the generic pattern output-type gate before their explicit compile arms. Other matrix-output pattern categories still error explicitly until the matrix arithmetic policy is resolved.
- Fixed: `hsvadjust` now uses the MaterialX default `amount=(0,1,1)` and carries the declared output type so color4 outputs preserve alpha. It previously defaulted amount to zero and always emitted color3.
- Fixed: `saturate` now preserves color4 alpha while applying luma interpolation to RGB only.
- Fixed: `colorcorrect` now follows the stdlib nodegraph order: hsv hue adjustment, saturation, range gamma, lift, gain, contrast, and exposure; color4 alpha is preserved. The previous direct implementation applied lift/gain/gamma/contrast in a different order and used `1 + contrast`.
- Fixed: `range` no longer silently substitutes `1e-30` for non-positive input spans; reversed ranges now evaluate through the actual `(inhigh - inlow)` denominator, and zero spans propagate floating-point infinities/NaNs instead of hiding invalid input.
- Fixed: `unpremult` now passes the complete color through unchanged when alpha is zero, matching the StandardNodes description. It previously divided RGB by `1e-6`, changing nonzero RGB at alpha zero.
- Fixed: value `mix` now supports component-wise mix inputs for vector/color outputs instead of forcing `mix` to a float. Float mix still broadcasts through the existing conversion helpers.
- Fixed: conditional pattern nodes now compile integer outputs and matrix outputs for ifgreater/ifgreatereq/ifequal, and boolean/integer/matrix outputs for ifelse, instead of being rejected by the generic pattern output gate.
- Fixed: `zero_param(Matrix33/Matrix44)` now returns a zero matrix, not identity. Matrix nodes with identity defaults continue to pass explicit identity defaults where the nodedef requires them.
- Fixed: `ifequal` value-output variants now use `value1=0` and `value2=0` defaults and preserve the declared comparison input type, rather than inheriting `ifgreater`'s `value1=1` default.
- Fixed: `switch` now preserves a declared integer `which` socket and supports matrix outputs. Runtime same-type matrix conversion now returns the matrix value instead of panicking.

## Fixes

- Fixed `src/material/mtlx_material.rs` front/back material flag handling:
  - `may_emit` now considers `back_compiled`.
  - `max_emission` now uses the max of front/back compiled materials.
  - `has_alpha_test` now considers front/back passthrough and opacity tests.
  - `any_hit` now evaluates passthrough/opacity-test flags on the active front/back compiled material.
- Fixed part of `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` silent fallback behavior:
  - `String`/`Filename`/unsupported `MtlxValue` literals no longer become `ParamRef::Float(0.0)`.
  - `String`/`Filename`/unsupported `MtlxValue` literals no longer become `Value::Empty`.
  - These now return `CompileError::Unsupported`.
- Fixed additional `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` silent fallback behavior:
  - Non-closure literal/string/geomprop inputs connected to closure sockets now return `CompileError::Unsupported` instead of `ClosureNode::Zero`.
  - Constant/geometric/surfacematerial/light nodes used as closures now error; displacement remains ignored with a warning under the allowed displacement exception.
  - Unknown `worleynoise*.style` values now error instead of becoming `distance`.
  - Unsupported image colorspaces now emit a warning before being treated as linear under the temporary sRGB/linear-only project constraint.
  - `triplanarblend.filtertype=cubic` now emits a warning before using linear filtering.
  - Rechecked `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.StandardNodes.md` lines 1158-1208, `lib/materialx/libraries/stdlib/stdlib_defs.mtlx` lines 4732-4762 and 5089-5092, `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/libraries/stdlib/genmdl/stdlib_genmdl_impl.mtlx` lines 706-708 and 755-771, and `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` lines 1664-1819, 2074-2270, 3000-3006, 3584-3607, 5101-5124, 5486-5506, 5734-5744, and 6220-6232.
- Fixed `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/types.rs` malformed array parsing:
  - Integer, float, vector, and color arrays now reject malformed elements instead of dropping them with `filter_map`.
- Fixed stale operand-layout comments in `src/material/mtlx/compiled.rs` for hextiled image, hextiled normal map, noise, randomfloat, randomcolor, and bump-with-frame.
- Fixed `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` closure sampling PDFs:
  - `Mix` samples now multiply the returned sample PDF by the chosen branch probability.
  - `Layer` samples now multiply the returned sample PDF by the selected top/base branch probability.
- Removed unused silent enum parsers in `src/bsdf/mtlx/dielectric.rs` and `src/bsdf/mtlx/sheen.rs` that mapped unknown `scatter_mode`/`mode` tokens to defaults.
- Fixed NEE/MIS direct-light gating in `src/integrator/nee.rs` and `src/integrator/mis.rs`:
  - Direct-light estimation no longer depends on the flags of a randomly selected BSDF sample.
  - Mixed MaterialX closures with delta and smooth components keep their direct-light estimator.
  - Pure emitters are still excluded from direct-light sampling.
- Fixed `src/scene.rs` MaterialX scratch capacity accounting to include `back_compiled.num_registers` as well as the front graph.
- Fixed parser strictness in `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/parser.rs`:
  - The root `version` attribute is required and malformed versions error.
  - Required element attributes such as `name`, `type`, `node`, and `nodedef` now error instead of becoming empty/default values or being skipped.
  - `parse_inputs` and `parse_outputs` no longer use `filter_map` to drop malformed sockets.
  - `xi:include` elements without `href` now error instead of being ignored.
  - Read/compared `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.Specification.md` lines 316-326 and `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.Supplement.md` lines 83-108 against `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/parser.rs` lines 256-288 and tests lines 1230-1248.
- Fixed nodedef/library strictness in `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/library.rs`:
  - Missing/cyclic inheritance is an error.
  - Requested versions must match exactly.
  - Requested version `major` and declared version `major.0` now match the MaterialX rule that an omitted minor version means zero.
  - Unknown node inputs reject the candidate nodedef.
  - Required standard library files must exist.
  - Read/compared `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.Specification.md` lines 938-1008 and 1031-1068 plus `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.Supplement.md` lines 83-108 against `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/library.rs` lines 1-525.
- Fixed flatten strictness in `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs`:
  - Invalid typed literals are errors except string/filename/geomname text.
  - Missing `interfacename` bindings are errors.
  - Invalid nodedef/nodegraph defaults are errors.
  - Missing nodegraph implementations for non-primitive resolved nodedefs are errors.
  - `disable` remains a node control input, not a nodedef data socket.
  - Local document nodedef version resolution now treats `major` and `major.0` as equivalent, matching the MaterialX version format rule.
  - Read/compared `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.Specification.md` lines 599-679, 938-1008, 1031-1068, and 1228-1280 against `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs` lines 1-1835 and tests lines 1984-2011 plus existing missing-interface/default regression tests.
- Fixed build-time image colorspace handling in `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/build.rs` to warn on unsupported or approximated colorspaces instead of silently falling back.
  - Read/compared `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.StandardNodes.md` lines 55-125 and `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.Specification.md` lines 558-572 against `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/build.rs` lines 1-590 and tests lines 674-680. Image/UDIM load failures are intentionally warnings so runtime image nodes can return their `default` values as specified.
- Fixed MaterialX math runtime behavior in `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` to match MDL native math for modulo and unary domain handling.
  - Rechecked `lib/materialx/libraries/stdlib/stdlib_defs.mtlx` lines 1422-1450, 1518-1538, 1573-1588, `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.StandardNodes.md` lines 55-125 and 618-621, `/tmp/MDL_spec_1.10.2.txt` lines 7560-7570 and 7608-7617, `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` lines 187-241, 1124-1151, 2400-2492, and 2675-2751, and `src/material/texture.rs` lines 329-360 and 468-472.
- Fixed MaterialX Chiang hair `cuticle_angle` range handling in `src/bsdf/mtlx/chiang_hair.rs`.
  - Rechecked `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/documents/Specification/MaterialX.PBRSpec.md` lines 248-258, `lib/materialx/libraries/pbrlib/pbrlib_defs.mtlx` lines 143-151, `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/source/MaterialXGenMdl/mdl/materialx/pbrlib_1_6.mdl` lines 444-467, and `src/bsdf/mtlx/chiang_hair.rs` lines 165-218 and 435-452.
- Fixed MaterialX `hextilednormalmap` default/frame handling in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`, `src/material/mtlx/compiled.rs`, and `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`.
- Fixed `surface_unlit` default handling in `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs` and `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`.
- Fixed PBR roughness utility handling in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` and `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`.
- Fixed ramp/split omitted right-bottom defaults in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`.
- Fixed Worley/unifiednoise enum socket handling in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`.
- Fixed boolean logical, geompropvalue integer/boolean, unsupported string/filename pattern output, and geomcolor default handling in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` and `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`.
- Fixed `safepower` and integer `floor`/`ceil`/`round` output handling in `src/material/mtlx/compiled.rs`, `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`, and `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`.
- Fixed vector dimension preservation for `magnitude`, `dotproduct`, and `distance` in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`.
- Fixed `convert` nodes with Boolean/Integer outputs and `creatematrix` Matrix33/Matrix44 outputs in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`.
- Fixed `creatematrix` Vector3-row Matrix44 behavior to match MDL row expansion with implicit w components `(0, 0, 0, 1)` in `src/material/mtlx/compiled.rs`, `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`, and `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`.
- Fixed `dot` organization nodes with Boolean, Integer, Matrix33, and Matrix44 outputs to compile as MaterialX/MDL passthrough nodes in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`.
- Fixed `generalized_schlick_bsdf` and `generalized_schlick_edf` to preserve `exponent` values below 1.0 instead of silently clamping them.
- Fixed MaterialX `add_bsdf` runtime behavior to match the MaterialX 1.39.4 MDL implementation's equal 0.5/0.5 BSDF mix in `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`, including sampling PMF, eval, pdf, directional albedo, and thin-walled transmittance traversal.
- Fixed `conical_edf.normal` handling in `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`; the socket is no longer ignored during `le` evaluation.
- Fixed `heighttonormal` constant-height behavior and removed the silent wrong dynamic-height approximation in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs`; dynamic height inputs now error until derivative/sample-grid evaluation is implemented.
- Fixed `extract` negative-index behavior in `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` to match generated MDL default-branch channel selection.
- Implemented MaterialX matrix `add/subtract/multiply/divide` compile/runtime support in `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` and `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs`.
- Added regression coverage that unsupported `blur` nodes fail explicitly instead of silently becoming passthrough.

- Read/compared for light tree closure audit:
  - `src/material/mtlx_material.rs` lines 218-245: `MtlxMaterial::light_tree_precompute` and `light_tree_importance`.
  - `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` lines 3136-3296 and 4290-4558: directional albedo, new light tree closure summary, per-closure lobe extraction.
  - `src/material/simple_pbr.rs` lines 420-455, `src/material/dielectric_ggx.rs` lines 310-340, and `src/material/standard_surface.rs` lines 640-670: existing diffuse/glossy/BTDF light tree lobe construction.
  - `src/light_tree/lobe.rs` lines 1-470 and `src/light_tree/importance.rs` lines 1-39: precompute fields, SG importance functions, glossy roughness merge, and material dispatch boundary.
  - `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/libraries/pbrlib/genmdl/pbrlib_genmdl_impl.mtlx` lines 13-20, 84-88: MDL binding for dielectric/conductor/generalized_schlick and roughness utility nodes.
  - `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/source/MaterialXGenMdl/mdl/materialx/pbrlib_1_9.mdl` lines 140-340 and `/tmp/materialx-1.39.4/source/MaterialXGenMdl/mdl/materialx/pbrlib_1_6.mdl` lines 140-340, 960-990: MDL roughness sockets are microfacet roughness/alpha inputs, dielectric/conductor/gschlick use GGX reflection/transmission modes, and roughness_anisotropy squares scalar roughness.
- Fixed: MaterialX light tree precompute no longer collapses all directional albedo into a diffuse proxy. It now preserves diffuse, glossy conductor/dielectric/gschlick, and dielectric/gschlick transmission as BTDF lobes where the local light-tree representation supports them, and `MtlxMaterial::light_tree_importance` sums diffuse/glossy/BTDF importance.
- Added tests:
  - `light_tree_precompute_keeps_conductor_as_glossy_lobe`
  - `light_tree_precompute_keeps_dielectric_transmission_as_btdf_lobe`

  - `cargo test spec_`
  - `cargo check`
- Read/compared for integrator/light transport coupling:
  - `src/integrator/pt.rs` lines 1-92: path throughput uses `sample.weight`, calls `precompute_shading` before `le`/`sample`, and counts environment/emissive hits without direct-light MIS.
  - `src/integrator/nee.rs` lines 1-150 and tests lines 152-330: direct-light sampling, `sample.weight` throughput, `le`, direct light PDF, and pure-emitter gating.
  - `src/integrator/mis.rs` lines 1-241 and tests lines 244-430: direct-light MIS, BSDF-sampled area/environment MIS, `sample.pdf`, `le`, and light-tree reverse PDF.
  - `src/light/mod.rs` lines 1-420: top-level category PMF, tree/environment/directional sampling, triangle/environment reverse PDFs.
  - `src/light/area.rs` lines 1-92, `src/light/environment.rs` lines 1-260, `src/light/point.rs` lines 1-140, `src/light/spot.rs` lines 1-190, and `src/light_tree/traversal.rs` lines 1-144: area solid-angle conversion, environment PDF, delta light PDF=1, light-tree leaf PMF and reverse PMF.
- Fixed: NEE and MIS direct-light contributions now use `abs(ns·wi)` after `material.eval` instead of clamping to the front hemisphere. This preserves MaterialX dielectric/gschlick BTDF direct lighting for lights below the surface while reflection-only materials still return zero from `eval`.
- Added tests:
  - `direct_light_nee_keeps_transmission_below_surface`
  - `direct_light_mis_keeps_transmission_below_surface`
- Verification:
  - Failed expectedly: `cargo test direct_light_nee_keeps_transmission_below_surface direct_light_mis_keeps_transmission_below_surface` because Cargo accepts one test-name filter.

- Read/compared for scene coupling:
  - `src/scene.rs` lines 1-260: scene state, material addition, instance registration, light sampler/tree rebuild, Mtlx scratch capacity including back material graphs.
  - `src/scene.rs` lines 260-620: `closest_hit` alpha-test path, scratch checkpoint/restore around `any_hit`, `alpha_test_shading_vertex`, `shading_vertex_from_triangle_sample`, `shading_vertex`, area-light PDF, area-light triangle registration from `may_emit/max_emission`.
  - `src/scene.rs` lines 620-980: mesh hit filter, shading differentials, cone fallback, finite derivative clamp, transform bounds, and scene test helpers.
  - Existing tests reviewed: closest hit transparency, shading vertex interpolation/normal map/differentials, area-light solid-angle PDF and non-emissive triangle rejection.
- Result: no new code change in this chunk. Current scene coupling matches the intended MaterialX behavior: alpha/passthrough is evaluated through `any_hit` without retaining scratch garbage; `front_face/ng/ns/frame` are built from geometric incident direction and prepared normals; emissive triangle registration uses conservative `may_emit/max_emission`; area-light reverse PDF remains uniform area density multiplied separately by light-tree leaf PMF.
- Read/compared for loader, parser, library, flatten reference resolution:
  - `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/types.rs` lines 1-620: raw MaterialX document model, value parsing, strict vector/matrix/array literal parsing, and unit/value representation.
  - `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/parser.rs` lines 1-980: XML attribute handling, namespace/include traversal, nodegraph/nodedef/material parsing, input/output parsing, and required-reference errors.
  - `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/library.rs` lines 1-620: nodedef/nodegraph/implementation registration, inherited nodedef resolution, version matching, and standard-library loading.
  - `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs` lines 1-1665: material root resolution, shaderref flattening, graph materialization, interface/default binding, unit conversion, disabled/passthrough handling, and existing flatten tests.
  - `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/build.rs` lines 1-320 and `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/mod.rs` lines 1-16: parse/flatten/compile boundary, front/back material compilation, texture collection handoff, and module exports.
- Fixed: missing `interfacename` on an input no longer becomes `FlatInput::Empty`. It now reports `FlattenError::Missing`, matching the no-silent-fallback rule for broken graph references.

- Fixed:
  - Shader-semantic `value=""` now materializes as `FlatInput::Empty` instead of an invalid literal error.
  - `surfacematerial` with omitted or explicitly empty `surfaceshader` compiles to an empty closure and is marked passthrough, so it is treated as transparent by `any_hit`.
  - Explicitly empty `backsurfaceshader` and `displacementshader` no longer trigger spurious materialization or displacement warnings.
  - `volumematerial` now emits a warning and flattens to an empty passthrough surface, matching the current no-volume-rendering constraint instead of hard erroring.

  - Node materialization cache keys include the requested output so different outputs of the same custom node cannot alias.
  - Local and library nodedef matching now considers multi-output definitions by checking that the requested node type is compatible with at least one declared output.

- Fixed:
  - `image`/`tiledimage`/`latlongimage` float outputs sampled from color textures now return the Red channel instead of ACEScg luminance, matching StandardNodes' "first N channels" rule.
  - `image` color/vector4 output from a color-only texture now fills the missing fourth channel with 0.0 instead of 1.0, matching the "channels beyond the file are zero" rule for non-alpha image data.
  - Scalar `image`/`tiledimage` with `<UDIM>` or `<UVTILE>` filenames now scans and loads scalar UDIM tiles instead of silently compiling as a missing texture. UDIM tiles can now carry an optional scalar pyramid alongside RGB/alpha, and runtime float output prefers the scalar tile.
  - Texture lookup now prefers scalar textures for float/integer image outputs, so using the same filename for both color and float images no longer makes the float node read a luminance-converted color texture.

- Fixed:
  - `normalmap` no longer uses zero vectors as a missing-socket sentinel for `normal`, `tangent`, or `bitangent`; missing frame sockets are now compiled to explicit world-space defaultgeomprop loads.
  - The default-frame path uses normalized geometric tangents/bitangents, matching the default `Tworld`/`Bworld` geometric values.
  - Both default-frame and explicit-frame paths now follow the MDL expression `normalize(tangent*x*scale.x + bitangent*y*scale.y + normal*z)`.
  - Raw zero input now maps to `(0,0,1)` before the same frame expression, matching MDL.

- Findings:
  - `surface` defaults matched the PBR nodedef (`bsdf=zero`, `edf=zero`, `opacity=1`, `thin_walled=false`), but `thin_walled` accepted non-boolean constant values and silently treated them as false.
  - `surface_unlit` defaults matched the nodedef (`emission=1`, `emission_color=1`, `transmission=0`, `transmission_color=1`, `opacity=1`, thin-walled material), but `transmission` was not saturated before computing `(1 - transmission)` and transmission color, unlike MDL `mx_surface_unlit`.
  - `surface_unlit` transmission is represented with the existing `Translucent` closure, but `MtlxMaterial::sample` converts thin-walled transmission samples to straight-through delta transmission. `eval`/`pdf` still exposed diffuse transmission density for opposite-side light directions, which is inconsistent with the delta path and MDL specular transmit behavior.
  - `volume` remains warning/pass-through/no-volume per current project constraint. `light` nodes remain unsupported in surface materials and error instead of silently falling back.
- Changes:

  - `surface_unlit.transmission` is clamped to `[0, 1]` before both emission attenuation and transmission-color multiplication.
  - `MtlxMaterial::eval` and `MtlxMaterial::pdf` now return zero for opposite-side thin-walled transmission directions, matching delta transmission handling used by `sample` and avoiding invalid NEE/MIS diffuse-transmission density for `surface_unlit`.
  - Added `spec_surface_unlit_saturates_transmission_like_mdl`.

- Findings:
  - `roughness_anisotropy`, `glossiness_anisotropy`, `roughness_dual`, `blackbody`, and `artistic_ior` matched the MDL formulas/defaults already in local runtime/tests.
  - `chiang_hair_roughness` did not match MDL: local runtime returned clamped/scaled input roughness, while MDL converts artistic longitudinal/azimuthal roughness to Chiang variance values and scales only the longitudinal variance for TT/TRT.
  - `deon_hair_absorption_from_melanin` did not match MDL: local runtime linearly mixed pigment colors, while MDL maps melanin concentration through `-log(1-concentration)` and pigment colors through `-log(color)`.
  - `chiang_hair_absorption_from_color` used an effectively unbounded lower/upper color clamp; MDL clamps scattering color to `[0.001, 1.0]` before the logarithm.
- Changes:
  - `Instruction::ChiangHairRoughness` now implements the MDL variance formula and TT/TRT scaling.
  - `Instruction::DeonHairAbsorptionFromMelanin` now implements the MDL logarithmic melanin/pigment mapping.

- Findings:
  - `bits_to_01` did not match MDL for hash values with the high bit set. MDL treats the hash as signed `int` and uses unsigned-right-shift-by-one divided by `2147483647` for negative values; local code divided the raw `u32` by `4294967295`.
  - `fractal2d/3d` forced at least one octave in both runtime operand handling and pattern functions, while MDL's `for (i < mxp_octaves)` loop returns zero for `octaves <= 0`.
  - Existing vector2 fractal offset-channel behavior, randomfloat/randomcolor cellnoise lowering, checkerboard UV offset, Worley style validation, and unifiednoise type/style handling remained consistent with the checked nodedef/MDL ranges after the above fixes.

  - `Instruction::Noise` now clamps runtime `octaves` to a minimum of `0` instead of `1`.

- Findings:
  - `modulo` already matched MDL `x - y * floor(x/y)`, and divide/sqrt/ln/asin/acos use raw floating-point operations without silent clamping.
  - `fract` used Rust `f32::fract()`, which returns a signed fractional part for negative inputs. MaterialX generated MDL uses `math::frac`, generated OSL uses `in - floor(in)`, and GLSL uses `fract`, so negative inputs must map to the positive fractional part.

  - `UnaryOp::Fract` now evaluates `x - floor(x)` for scalar, vector, and color channels through the existing unary mapping.

- Initial finding, closed by the follow-up implementation below:
  - Local EDF evaluation stores emission as already evaluated radiance (`shape * color`) and implements `add_edf` as radiance addition. MaterialX PBR docs describe additive EDF blending, which this matches physically.
  - The latest generated MDL implementation (1.7 and re-exported by 1.9/1.10) represents EDF shape and `material_emission.intensity` separately: `mx_add_edf` uses `df::unbounded_mix` of the EDF shapes with weights `1.0, 1.0` and separately adds intensities. This is explicitly documented in MDL comments as a workaround because adding EDFs is not directly supported.
  - This was not yet MDL-equivalent for mixed EDF shapes with different intensities before the follow-up implementation below.
- Follow-up implementation for EDF shape-and-intensity handling:
  - `src/material/mtlx/runtime.rs` lines 4867-5038 after the change were checked line by line. `evaluate_edf` now routes through `EdfTerms { shape, intensity }`, with uniform/conical EDFs producing unit distribution shape and color intensity, generalized Schlick modifying shape while preserving intensity, `mix_edf` mixing shape and intensity separately, `add_edf` adding shape and intensity separately, and `multiply_edf` scaling intensity. Conditionals, switch, and surface EDF delegation preserve the selected terms.
  - `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` lines 6141-6247 after the change were checked line by line. `closure_max_emission` now uses the same shape/intensity decomposition for conservative emission bounds, so MDL-style `add_edf` no longer underestimates `max_emission` for light-tree/emitter registration.
  - `src/material/mtlx/spec_tests.rs` lines 6995-7112 after the change were checked line by line for EDF add runtime and max-emission regression coverage.
  - `src/material/mtlx/compile.rs` lines 1625-1632 after the change were checked line by line; the redundant `operand_to_vreg` comment was removed under the no-unnecessary-comments rule.
- Changes:
  - Added `EdfTerms` decomposition in EDF runtime evaluation.
  - Changed EDF `mix`, `add`, `multiply`, generalized Schlick, and branch traversal to match the generated MDL shape/intensity model instead of pre-combined radiance math.
  - Updated `closure_max_emission` to use a matching shape/intensity bound.
  - Added `spec_add_edf_matches_mdl_unbounded_shape_and_intensity_add`.
  - Added `spec_add_edf_max_emission_matches_mdl_shape_intensity_bound`.

- Findings and fixes:
  - XIncluded child documents without their own root `namespace` or `colorspace` did not inherit the including document's hierarchical root attributes before merge. The parser now applies parent namespace and colorspace to included children when the child root omits them.
  - Document/root namespace application qualified nodedef names, references, nodegraph names, implementations, and node uses, but not `RawNodeDef.node`; `node` is now qualified as required by the namespace section.
  - Element-level `<nodedef namespace="...">` and `<nodegraph namespace="...">` are now applied exactly to nodedef `name`/`node` and nodegraph `name`. The nodegraph `nodedef` reference remains explicit, matching the rule that element namespace does not qualify external references.
  - A transient local change also applied element-level `namespace` to `<implementation>`, but implementation attributes in the specification do not include `namespace`; this was reverted so only document-scope namespace qualification affects implementation names and references.

  - `cargo fmt`

  - `cargo check`

  - `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/resolver.rs` lines 1-150, `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/library.rs` lines 175-288 and 430-535, and `src/scene_loader/mtlx_loader/flatten.rs` lines 1122-1245 after the change were checked line by line.
- Finding and fix:
  - An explicit `nodedef="..."` on a node instance was accepted by name alone in the resolver. A node could therefore reference a nodedef whose declared `node` category or signature did not match the instance, silently selecting the wrong implementation path.
  - Library nodedef signature matching is now shared with the resolver, and explicit nodedef resolution errors when the nodedef's `node` category, input names/types, or output type do not match the node use.
  - Local document nodedef lookup in the flattener now applies the same category/signature gate, and a local explicit nodedef mismatch errors instead of falling through to a library nodedef with the same name.
- Confirmed:
  - Target-specific nodedefs and nodegraphs remain excluded from the universal renderer path.
  - Existing inherited library nodedefs still load: `standard_surface` 1.0.1 has its own implementation entry reusing the 1.0.0 graph, and `UsdUVTexture` 2.2 has its own nodegraph while inheriting inputs from 2.3.

  - `cargo fmt`

- Findings and fixes:
  - `parse_recursive` previously parsed parent contents first and appended XIncluded contents later. Upstream MaterialX imports included documents before constructing the parent element tree, and local later-wins nodedef maps made the old order observable for duplicate definitions. The parser now merges included documents first and then merges the including document's own contents.
  - Parent root `fileprefix` was not inherited by XIncluded children whose own root omitted `fileprefix`; local parsing eagerly prefixes filename inputs, so the inherited prefix must be passed while parsing the included child. The recursive parser now passes the active root prefix into included documents unless they define their own root prefix.
  - Element-scoped `fileprefix` was incomplete: node/nodedef/material scopes and input-level `fileprefix` now feed filename input parsing, while inner scopes override outer scopes as specified.
  - Removed a redundant `fileprefix` parser comment under the no-unnecessary-comments rule.
